# RESEARCH_SA15 research report

AI-produced by GPT-6 Astra (OpenAI). No claim of priority. All supplied manuscript witnesses are AI transcriptions; no scans were available. This is a scheduled checkpoint of active work, not a completed response.

## Proof and source record

The paper contains the full current proofs. `agents/*/search_log.md`, `SEARCH_REFERENCE_LOG.md`, and `notes.md` retain exact searches, source locations and failed approaches. The bibliography marks acquisition state as records are audited; completeness of the source census remains F.2. Sources downloaded for checking are omitted from this ZIP; their locations are retained in the bibliography and source logs.

## Computations

- `code/k3_lift_check.py`: exact characteristic-two Groebner certificates for all six affine charts of the complete intersection K3, involution invariance, elliptic discriminant, and Hensel root through 2^32. Output `outputs/k3_lift_check.json`, 0.261261 seconds. Does not compute the final hyperplane surface equation; the proof gives a terminating enumeration.
- `agents/monodromy/code/plane_char2.py`: explicit quintic smoothness, Hasse-Witt rank and finite-field counts through F64. Inputs and scope in retained code; exact output in `agents/monodromy/outputs/plane_char2.json`.
- `agents/monodromy/code/quintic_group_checks.py`: conic restriction rank, stabilizer maximal-subgroup order divisibility, orbit exclusions and cocycle linear algebra. Output `quintic_group_checks.json`; 0.01259 seconds.
- `agents/reduction_descent/code/verify_p3_full_torsion.py`: rational 3-torsion coordinates, all 81 group sums, discriminant and invariant checks; output JSON beside code.
- `agents/obstructions_crystals/code/splitting_check.py`: explicit positive splitting groups and exact linear inconsistency certificates for (d,p)=(3,3),(4,2); output and certificate JSON beside code; approximately 0.17 seconds.
- `code/build_paper.py`: reproducible LuaLaTeX/BibTeX build and final-log overflow inventory.

## Corrections and audit cautions

1. A.1: the prompt says there is no section 8.2. Both supplied witnesses contain a deleted 8.2; we record this without claiming scan verification. The transposed torsion map involves Gysin and cup product with d times the fixed polarization, so the factor d cannot be dropped.
2. R1/R2: independently checked in A_foundations. Stable coherent restriction does not itself prove full coherent injectivity.
3. A.6: torsion-free generic p-adic cohomology does not imply zero special stable crystalline torsion without a ramification/range condition. The exact specialization kernel is retained.
4. R3(b): the unverified Kisin sketch is replaced by an elementary complete torsion-extension/twist construction; no unchecked dictionary is used for B.1.
5. R4: the nonreduced equivalence assertion is false. A fresh issue in its reduction 100:15-17 is that EGA finite-presentation spreading does not automatically spread an infinite p-divisible-group isomorphism; repair remains active.
6. R5: splitting lists checked with retained exact certificates; the symplectic supplement/complement distinction is explicit.
7. Lead D: full finite monodromy does not imply a nonzero coherent H2 obstruction on every proper base; on curves H2 vanishes.
8. Lead C: negative-one gluing alone is not a counterexample, because finite étale twisting can be effective.
9. E.7 is now independently audited: alpha=0 does not imply equality of the full lattices. E.10(a) is refuted integrally on the literal site; comparisons for a different modern site have not been substituted.
10. The B.2 list omits defect twelve in residue characteristic three. An explicit full-rational-3-torsion example over Q3(i,zeta3) proves the correction.
11. The E.9 product formula needs the Koszul factor (-1)^(gh); its genus-two case refutes the literal multiplicativity claim.

## Checkpoint 03: current conclusions superseding earlier checkpoint descriptions

The paper now proves every-degree odd-characteristic monodromy for hypersurfaces in every projective space, with exact geometric and arithmetic images and the sharp every-open residue threshold d=n+2. The proof includes independent restriction monodromy, the modular hook-module calculation, quadric reflections, a quartic seed, degree induction, and an explicit valuation-one determinant family. Characteristic-two higher-dimensional projective spaces are under active investigation.

Part C has materially changed: reducedness does not repair the claimed equivalence. The ring in C-RD11 is reduced, noetherian, geometrically unibranch, with a nonfinite normalization; its elliptic equation has constant split p-divisible data after normalization but a nonconstant j-invariant on the dual-number closed fibre. C.4 is settled for the requested base categories, including a complete infinite-node counterexample and invariance of effectivity under reduction. E.10 now has a proper counterexample as well as the affine ones. E.2 has a proved coherent characteristic-zero subcase, with its full relative ordinary-module claim still open.

All new mathematical claims above underwent independent root audit. The finite equation and local-ring identities were checked by retained programs. Infinite field choices and terminating geometric enumeration constructions are mathematical constructions; the package does not claim that those infinite constructions or surface-equation searches were numerically executed. The current item table governs the current status; previous checkpoint narratives remain a historical record. The full prompt-named bibliography census and exact statement-to-manuscript mapping are still being completed.

### Checkpoint 03 final mathematical update

The independent audit also completed the elementary characteristic-two proof in every projective dimension. Consequently A.4 is now completely determined for X=P^n for every p,n,d; A.5 has the exact threshold d=n+2 for every characteristic. The proof uses independent Artin-Schreier point-pair covers, Johnson-graph coordinate transpositions, elementary matrix generation and a globally descended Hasse-Witt covector. These statements remain special cases of the requested general-ambient questions.

## Checkpoint 04: current findings and access record

A.5 is now proved in the stated generality, with an explicit degree bound for every smooth projective connected ambient variety over a finite field. The proof independently realizes many node-permutation factors, lifts the finite part of the boundary Lang kernel through a henselian pair, bounds all discarded and newly appearing cohomology, and isolates a single alternating factor acting trivially on the error spaces. Its traces exhaust F_p. The point-count conclusion uses the exact constant-field/Chebotarev direction. This does not identify the full arbitrary-X monodromy group.

B.4 now has a finite universal upper bound, using the opened Kisin rational classification (including p=2), a bounded good-reduction extension, and the Vasiu–Zink finite-flat Hom-loss bound. Honda height-two formal groups give explicit lower bounds at every admissible ramification index. The sharp least general-height truncation is still open. The proposed p=2 height-one full-inertia calculation was rejected because the requested character is restricted to prime-to-p elements; the correct least height-one index is zero for every prime.

C.1 now has an absolute necessary and sufficient essential-image criterion on every locally noetherian base. It separately retains formal algebraization, actual punctured-completion comparison and compatible quasi-affine charts; it does not assume the desired global extension. In relative dimension one, canonical polarized algebraization removes the algebraization and atlas conditions. The additional nilpotent-overlap criterion is also exact once the reduced triple is realized. E.2 now includes all quasi-coherent modules on F-pure finite-type perfect-field schemes, while the general relative assertion remains open. The transcription's coherent/locally-noetherian insertion at6:7 is explicitly quoted and distinguished from the broader prompt.

The numbered statement register now covers all numbered environments, including previously unlabelled remarks and the new manuscript companion. The source census is passage-specific: Kisin, Messing, Mazur–Messing, Serre and Fontaine originals were obtained after earlier failed attempts. Original BBMII, Sah and the cotangent-complex books remain marked not obtained. The full SGA4½ target was not obtained; the trace formula used is the independently opened Katz SGA7II statement. No original scan reading is claimed.

# Additional primary-source searches and checks, 19 November 2026

## A.1 and A.3–A.5

Queries included `"Lefschetz" "H^1" "very ample" "characteristic p" Picard scheme restriction injective`, `"Picard" "restriction" "infinitesimal" "ample divisor" characteristic`, `"restriction" "Picard scheme" "ample" "injective" characteristic smooth`, and `"H^1" "restriction map" "smooth hyperplane" positive characteristic`. Bhatt–de Jong, arXiv:1302.3189, Theorems0.1–0.2, Example1.25, Theorem2.1, Lemmas2.5/2.7 and Corollary2.6 were opened. Their local Picard assertions and Frobenius-triviality results do not establish the smooth projective coherent restriction-kernel claim. Cotner–Zavyalov Definition2.2.1 and Theorem2.2.3 support the two all-degree positive ranges. Lauritzen–Rao's Kodaira counterexamples do not alone provide the needed nonzero restriction kernel.

For the general ambient variety the further searches were `"hypersurface sections" "p-rank" monodromy`, `"linear system" "p-rank" generic surface`, and `"point counts" "modulo p" "hypersurface" "every"`. No exact previously published arbitrary-X monodromy classification or matching residue theorem was located in these searches. This is a search report, not a priority assertion. The general residue proof uses the independently checked boundary construction; Entin's Proposition3.1, arXiv:1805.05454v3 pp7–8, supplies the exact product-symmetric criterion. Stacks Lemmas41.20.5 (0BTN) and58.8.2 (09ZS) were opened and their proofs checked for the henselian finite-part construction. The earlier monodromy log contains the plane, ordinary-locus, unit-root, theta and projective-space searches.

## B.1–B.4

The reduction agent's SOURCE_RECORD.tsv records the searches for full p-torsion with additive reduction, tame and wild semistability defects, Drinfeld-level deformation rings, and bounded finite-flat Hom extension. The originals of Kisin's Crystalline representations and F-crystals and Moduli of finite flat group schemes and modularity were eventually obtained: earlier not-obtained entries are retained only as dated search history. Root directly checked Kisin Lemma2.2.4 and Corollary2.2.6, including the prime2 rational distinction, and Vasiu–Zink Boundedness Theorem1/Corollary2 and its explicit loss bound.

Queries `Fontaine representations l adiques potentiellement semi stables Weil Deligne 1994 Astérisque 223 pdf` and original Numdam retrieval obtained Fontaine ExposésIII andVIII. Root read VIII1.1.1–1.1.2,1.3.5, Theorem2.3.2, Remark2.3.3,2.3.7 and III5.1.4–5.1.7. The p-adic Weil action on printed p329 was visually inspected. These sources verify the corrected potentially semistable dictionary; they do not turn the manuscript's weaker filtered condition into potential semistability.

## C.1–C.4

The reduction agent's records cover Faltings–Chai ChapterV, Vasiu–Zink purity, Grothendieck's normal-base extension theorem, proper descent, and local systems over nonnormal or nonexcellent bases. Root opened Moret-Bailly1996 Theorem0.3 p561 and Vasiu–Zink Lemma24 p590, then checked the complete-base nilpotent lifting and overlap criterion. The counterexample with nonfinite normalization is an independent ring construction; it is not inferred from failure to locate a theorem. ## D.1–D.2 and E.9

Exact-title and DOI searches for Sah's Cohomology of split group extensions did not obtain its original full text. Serre's Abelian l-adic representations and elliptic curves was obtained in the1989 reprint, and ChapterIV3.4 Lemma3 and Exercises1–3 were read. The distinction between proper supplements and complements was rechecked. The obstruction agent's SEARCH_REFERENCE_LOG.md records the complete co-Lie, Artin–Schreier, wild free-quotient, polarization-normalization and canonical alpha_A searches. No source is claimed to contain the literal alpha_A formula unless an exact opened passage is supplied there.

## Named background sources and failed access

The four supplied original sources by Bhatt–Scholze, Scholze–Weinstein and Bhatt were opened at their introductory comparison/classification theorems; they are not substitutes for a missing finite-level dictionary. Messing and Mazur–Messing originals were eventually obtained and checked by the obstruction agent after the initial publisher-only attempts. The original Berthelot–Breen–MessingII and Illusie cotangent-complex volumes were not obtained through the tested Springer full-PDF endpoints. SGA4½ and SGA7I archive indices and page directories were opened; full PDFs exceeded web retrieval size or direct retrieval returned403. The exact SGA7I assertions used were read in an online transcription, with this limitation preserved. The point-count congruence was checked in Katz's original SGA7II ExposéXXII instead. No AI working edition found in search was substituted for an unopened original.


Late checkpoint04 audit: EGAIII1 original Theorem5.4.1 (printed pp156–157) and Theorem5.4.5 (pp157–158), opened indexed full PDF lines6710–6739, verify proper formal full faithfulness and ample formal algebraization in C13/C14. Root read and checked the complete proofs after locally noetherian and elliptic-height qualifications. The ordinary-abelian candidate and its new explicit computations are retained as pending independent computational audit; they are not claimed in checkpoint04 paper.

## Checkpoint 05: new results and exact scope

### A.1: a full coherent restriction counterexample in every prime

The unrestricted coherent assertion is now refuted. Starting with the explicitly described Lauritzen–Rao sixfold R, we independently compute its canonical bundle and identify H5(R,L^{-1}) with the dual of a concrete polynomial cokernel. The apolar functional based on (u0*z1-u1*z0)^(p-2) proves nonvanishing for every prime, rather than inferring a general statement from samples. A cyclic cover y^q=b, with q=2 for odd p and q=3 for p=2, turns that nonzero class into an actual restriction kernel. The pullback of L is very ample: the cover is the hypersurface y^q=B in the projective cone, which excludes the vertex. The branch is a fully specified generic form in 896 ambient coordinates over a specified perfect field. The Jacobian and Bertini arguments prove both smoothness assertions. Multiplication by y is an identity between two direct summands of the finite cyclic algebra, so the restriction kernel is nonzero. This also works for every smooth ay+s with a nonzero.

The failure occurs at degree one for this fixed embedding. The previously proved explicit high-degree bound still gives injectivity. The exact intermediate list, the surface case and the integral crystalline assertions are not inferred from this example and remain explicit obligations. The earlier report's statement that no actual coherent kernel had been verified is superseded.

### A.3–A.4: exact product invariants and effective full images

For X equal to a product of n ordinary elliptic curves with product polarization of degrees a_i>=3, the generic relative rank is d^n*product(a_i)-1 for every d>=1. If p divides every a_i*d, the entire geometric invariant covector space has dimension n for odd p and 2^n-1 for p=2. These are equalities, not lower bounds. The proof extends a covector across parameter-space divisors, identifies its lift with an endomorphism of the section space modulo scalars, and solves the Cartier identities using the character decomposition of Frobenius. A proposed intermediate shortcut asserting that Cartier roots preserve character spaces was rejected. The repaired proof raises the identity to the pth power, uses an eigenvector and the linear parameter coefficient, and proves character-space preservation instead. The correction is recorded as part of the audit.

Over F3 the explicit surface built from y²=x³+x²+1 has relative rank9d²-1 and exactly two arithmetic and geometric invariant covectors in every degree. Over F2 the analogous explicit ordinary product has exactly three in every positive even degree. Exact finite calculations give smooth ordinary sections in degrees one and two. The full image on the kernel of these fixed quotients is not claimed to be determined structurally.

A separate theorem gives a terminating exact algorithm for the complete geometric and arithmetic matrix groups from equations over a finite field, for every d>=max(1,reg(omega_X)-1). It constructs the finite étale algebra of Frobenius-fixed frames, obtains a primitive element from a bounded grid, factors by bounded finite polynomial enumeration, and tests every matrix by exact ideal preservation. Extending constants by degree |GL_r(Fp)| suffices to isolate the geometric components. Thus the construction also decides irreducibility. A fully specified extension to a finitely generated field of definition computes finite groups H_geom normal in H0 and a finite Galois constant field E. For any perfect base k, the arithmetic image is exactly rho^{-1}(Gal(E/(E intersection k))). The only residual field datum is that finite intersection; no universal computation over an unpresented noncomputable field is asserted. The report deliberately distinguishes this effective determination from a uniform description of all outputs as familiar finite groups. The latter remains the A.4 structural gate.

### B.3–B.4 and E.2

The higher wild characteristic-three argument proves that the maximum primitive formal valuation is divisible by three. It uses the Hesse-pencil cube-discriminant identity and a lower-ramification leading-coefficient argument. The resulting exact global minimum is e_min(n)=2*3^(2n-2). At each fixed e the largest possible bad full-torsion level lies between two explicit adjacent integers; except for the displayed one-level gap the answer is exact. For full9 the remaining indices are precisely24,30,42,48. This is a strictly smaller gate than the previous unrestricted higher-wild problem.

At p=2,e=1, the curve y²=x(x-1)(x+3) over Q2(zeta3) has E[4] isomorphic to mu4 direct sum Z/4, while its prime-to-2 inertia character differs from that of the split good group. Its discriminant valuation8 forces good-reduction ramification divisible by3, and the order-three trace is -1 instead of2. Therefore even manuscript level1 fails to determine the character in every height at least2. The precise minimum beyond this lower bound is still open.

For E.2, the characteristic-zero result extends to all flat quasi-coherent coefficients by a finite nilpotent filtration argument in the derived category. In characteristic p, a separate theorem covers all quasi-coherent coefficients on integral CM varieties with a smooth finite universal-homeomorphism cover. This includes the cusp k[t²,t³], whose Frobenius is not pure for any prime. The original Ben-Zvi–Nevins statements, including their ordinary pullback convention, were reopened and checked. General relative bases and arbitrary nonflat characteristic-zero coefficients remain open.

### Computational scope and independent checks

`code/coherent_restriction_counterexample.py` passed in0.668855 seconds. It checks all relevant monomial incidence relations for p=2,3,5,7,11,13, the characteristic-two40-to35 matrix of rank34, and cyclic multiplication. Generic smoothness and the all-prime conclusion use the written proofs. The root rerun of `ordinary_abelian_surface.py` took14.158152 seconds and verified four unit Jacobian ideals, Cartier rank36, relative rank35, four independent character components and seven F2-points. The degree-one certificate records the equation and Cartier matrix separately. The root rerun of `product_surface_obstruction.py` took0.047037 seconds and checked the F3 curve and its rational three-torsion.

The root independently read and reran `lang_frame_quartic.py` in0.186606 seconds. For the genuine family x4+y4+z4+x²yz+xz³+txy³+xyz², it verifies all three smoothness charts, its ordinary Hasse matrix, a six-dimensional frame algebra, all six GL2(F2) substitutions and a primitive polynomial. A simple Artin–Schreier pole proves geometric irreducibility, giving exactly diag(1,GL2(F2)) for this restricted family. This is not a claim about the full plane-quartic parameter space. The B.4 certificate checks all16 torsion points, all256 sums and all256 Galois compatibilities with exact arithmetic; a separate independent agent rerun passed. The wild-bound program checks the integer reductions within its stated finite range; it supplements the general proof.

All new theorem promotions follow independent proof checks. The paper and table retain six open main rows with narrower gates. Checkpoint production is not counted as mathematical movement, and the next scheduled substantive review remains due at06:50:40 UTC.


# Additional source and approach record for checkpoint 05

## A.1: why the new coherent counterexample answers a stronger question

The continuing searches concerned smooth very ample Kodaira counterexamples, restriction on the Picard scheme, Frobenius-killed coherent classes, and integral crystalline weak Lefschetz. Original full texts inspected include Lauritzen–Rao (1997), Section2 and its claim on pp3–5; Mukai's RIMS1736 (December2011), Theorems1–3; Totaro (2019), introduction and Theorem3.1; Ekedahl (1988), Proposition2.14 p120; Biswas–Holla, arXiv math/0603299v2, Section2 and Remark2.4; and Cotner–Zavyalov's Examples3.3.6/3.4.6. The exact endpoints and limitations are in the bibliography and census. These searches did not by themselves produce a verified smooth very ample surface with the required nonzero map. In particular negative line-bundle cohomology on a rational homogeneous variety cannot alone produce a kernel in its vanishing structure-sheaf cohomology.

The successful next approach was to change the ambient variety. A finite cyclic cover supplies a second summand in its structure-sheaf cohomology, and multiplication by the new homogeneous coordinate maps onto that summand identically. The explicit cone embedding verifies very ampleness. The all-prime cohomology of the sixfold was re-proved by an apolar functional, rather than delegated to a numerical sample or an unstated representation-theory result. This construction is credited as a derivation in this dossier using the opened Lauritzen–Rao example, without a priority claim. The original surface and full integral questions are retained.

Nakkajima–Yobuko arXiv1902.09110v3 was downloaded and read at Theorem6.10, hypotheses6.5 and Remark6.11, printed pp52–53. It supplies a precisely qualified integral weak-Lefschetz statement and a corrected large-degree proof. The lift/log hypotheses and the min(dim,p) range prevent using it to close the remaining arbitrary low-degree crystalline gate. The report does not silently remove those hypotheses.

## A.3–A.4: fixed covectors and a finite exact algorithm

Root reopened Sannai–Tanaka arXiv1411.5294v5 Lemma4.5 p11 and checked its Frobenius decomposition for ordinary abelian varieties. The product proof separately verifies ordinary SNC degeneration, all-degree normal generation, extension of invariant covectors across parameter divisors, and the exact character relations. The attempted assertion that taking a Cartier root preserves a Frobenius character space was rejected during audit. The revised proof establishes preservation by comparing a pth-power identity at an eigenvector and its first-order variation. This repair is essential to the exact invariant-space count.

The full-image algorithm was audited as an actual terminating procedure. No source was substituted for an unspecified Galois-group oracle. Its finite-field factorization step is bounded enumeration; its finitely generated field step is a shifted norm and square-free resultant; its absolute-factor step is a bounded coefficient ansatz followed by a proved terminating search in finite separable extensions. Root opened Stacks Tags037P,037Q,030W,030X, namely Lemmas10.47.8,10.47.13,10.44.2,10.44.3, and checked the needed finiteness, separability and geometric-integrality assertions. The finite field E is recovered from normalized factor coefficients, and its embedding in the selected frame component is computed by linear algebra.

The exact quartic-family test is a genuine smooth family, with three affine singularity ideals checked. The six-dimensional frame algebra has all six GL2(F2) actions, and a simple Artin–Schreier pole proves that it stays a field over algebraically closed constants. The algorithm gives an exact effective answer; a structural identification of all general-ambient groups is a distinct remaining objective. The product examples are now a concrete next test of that objective.

## B.3–B.4 and E.2

Anema–Top–Tuijp (2018), original SIGMA paper, Theorem1.1 p2 and Proposition5.1 pp4–5, was reopened. The former identifies the symplectic full-three-torsion Hesse family, and the latter gives the cube ratio of discriminants. Those exact statements support the invariant-differential step in the wild valuation argument. Root and the crystals agent independently checked the ramification calculation. The height-two level-four characteristic-two counterexample also passed an independent exhaustive group-law certificate. The reduction agent's log records its primary potential-good and supersingular-automorphism locators.

For E.2, Ben-Zvi–Nevins, original arXiv math/0212094v3, was reopened at Remark2.7 (pro-category tensor conventions), Proposition2.11, Theorem3.11, Theorem4.3 and Remark4.5. These identify ordinary pullback for the left-module/stratification equivalence; the claim is not inferred from their separate !-crystal result. Stacks Tag0CNF, Lemma29.47.9, was reopened for finite universal homeomorphisms and Frobenius powers. The arbitrary-flat characteristic-zero extension was independently checked against the previously opened Gaitsgory–Rozenblyum Corollary2.2.2 and Proposition3.4.3. A nilpotent filtration proves the ordinary flatness of every resulting value without a finite-generation assumption.

The full relative E.2 assertion is still under investigation. Ordinary tensor products cannot be replaced by completed tensor products in a descent argument without justification. In particular completion along the diagonal need not be faithfully flat over the uncompleted tensor product, so the tempting completed-nerve shortcut has not been used to claim a general theorem.

## Checkpoint 06: subsequent results and corrections

The table at the beginning of this report is the current status. Earlier checkpoint narratives below are dated research history; their former gates are superseded by the current table and this section. The work remains INCOMPLETE. In particular the broad E.2 assertion is now refuted, whereas the narrower manuscript insertion about a locally Noetherian base and coherent modules remains explicitly unresolved as a refinement.

### B.3: complete full-nine-torsion range

For p=3, an additive elliptic curve with full rational nine-torsion exists over a complete discretely valued field of absolute ramification e and perfect residue field if and only if 6 divides e and e>=18. All examples are over finite extensions of Q3. At the endpoint, E0:y²=x³-x over Q3(i) has division field L=Q3(i,E0[9]) of ramification72; its Z3[i]-module structure and exact primitive formal valuation1/72 prove this degree without an assumed local reciprocity dictionary. Taking the fixed field of the scalar i gives e18. A primitive formal parameter u transforms by i, and y²=x³-u4*x descends with all nine-torsion rational and exact defect4.

For every e=6d>18 the field is exactly K=Q3(i,eta,pi), where eta is a primitive ninth root and pi^d=eta-1. Its residue field is F9 and pi is a uniformizer. The displayed quadratic Hensel lift and a separately rescaled cubic Hensel lift supply three cube roots of (t-theta)/9 for theta=1,zeta,zeta². Fisher's original full-level equations and moduli interpretation then give E_t[9]=mu9 direct sum Z/9. The curve is y²+3txy+(t³-1)y=x³, with v(t)=9-e, v(j)=27 and v(Delta)=81-6e congruent9 modulo12. Thus it is potentially good but additive. The four former gaps e24,30,42,48 all have defect12. The proof explicitly avoids the invalid unrescaled Hensel inequality v(error)>2v(3); its integral rescaling has a simple linear reduction. The entire construction has independent parent and obstructions/crystals audits, with an independently executed exact symbolic certificate.

The fixed-e higher wild problem therefore starts at full27 and e216. Its previously proved one-level numerical bound and the sharp global ramification minimum remain valid. Higher-dimensional potentially good sharp thresholds remain open; the nine-torsion theorem does not assert them.

### E.2: counterexamples and relative positive theorem

Let I=Fp[t,t^-1]/Fp[t] and R=Fp[t] semidirect I with I²=0. Set s_n=(p^n-1)/(p-1), b_n=[t^-s_n] in I and X=Spec R[x]/(x^p-tx). The base is fully specified and non-Noetherian. The morphism is finite free of rankp, flat, finitely presented, and all its fibres are complete intersections. On any nilpotent test U inside T, choose n killing the p^n powers of its ideal and a local lift z of x. Multiplication by b_n*z^(p^n) defines a morphism delta of the structure crystal. Its definition is independent of every choice because t^(p^n)b_(n+1)=b_n. It is zero on X but nonzero on the finite free square-zero thickening T=Spec R[x]/(x^p-tx)^2, where it equals b1*x^p. As delta²=0, 1+delta is a nonidentity automorphism inducing the identity after restriction to stratifications. This is a direct proof on every infinitesimal test, independent of the Frobenius-divided comparison.

The same construction with the regular Noetherian base R0=Fp[t] and coefficient crystal O_T tensor_R0 I gives a nonzero morphism O_inf to that quasi-coherent crystal with zero restriction. This stronger variant uses noncoherent coefficients. It does not settle the manuscript's separate inserted restriction to a locally Noetherian base and coherent coefficients. That insertion is quoted from the supplied transcription, and the absent scans are not claimed to have been inspected.

The relative positive theorem proves that, over any Fp-algebra R, quasi-coherent infinitesimal crystals on a locally finitely presented X/R are equivalent to relative Frobenius-divided systems. When every relative Frobenius twist is pure, they are equivalent to ordinary stratified quasi-coherent modules. The proof uses finite free Frobenius on affine space, relative nilpotent envelopes, and effective pure module descent; it does not assume that R is perfect, reduced or Noetherian. As a concrete singular family, R[x,y]/(xy) satisfies the criterion for every Fp-algebra R via an explicit relative-Frobenius splitting.

### A.4 algorithm clarification and remaining structural work

In the absolute-factor search of the exact perfect-field monodromy algorithm, the proposed factor has positive degree but the complementary factor is now explicitly allowed to be a unit. This includes the full-degree absolute factor and repairs a missing branch in the earlier wording. It does not change the construction's output, proof strategy or arithmetic constant-field formula. The clarification is recorded rather than concealed as an unchanged proof.

### A.4: actual full images in two degrees on a fixed abelian surface

For E:y²+xy=x³+1 over F2 and X=E×E with L=O_E(3O) external tensor O_E(3O), the geometric and arithmetic groups both equal GL8(F2) in degree one. In degree two both equal the entire pointwise stabilizer of the three previously constructed covectors, namely the matrices [A B;0 I3] with A in GL32(F2) and B an arbitrary32-by3 binary matrix. In particular the translation subgroup has order2^96. These conclusions persist over every perfect extension of F2. The fixed geometric base point and Fp-structure remain those of the relative Lang local system; dual and inverse-Frobenius conventions are specified in the proof.

The argument certifies all divisorial inertia rather than extrapolating from point samples. On the whole projective linear system, including singular sections, the relative coherent bundle is the cotangent bundle and its Frobenius is represented by an explicitly constructed linear Cartier matrix. A line with ordinary leading coefficient meets every geometric determinant component. Exact finite-field ranks of M and M^[2]M at every root on the line prove stable rank loss exactly one at every generic Hasse divisor. A henselian finite Lang subgroup then fixes a hyperplane, so characteristic-two inertia consists of transvections. Purity and the absence of connected finite etale covers of projective space prove generation by these transvections.

Irreducible nonreciprocal Frobenius polynomials of degrees8 and32 supply primes17 and65537, respectively. An elementary socle argument and the corresponding prime-order exclusion rule out proper block decompositions. The opened Kantor–Seress Theorem3.6 then leaves the full linear group: every other classified case preserves a unique alternating form, incompatible with the nonreciprocal polynomial. ### A.4: degree three and two all-degree structural results

The later audited degree-three certificate gives geometric and arithmetic group GL80(F2). The same base certificate is used in an all-degree proof. Cubes of a very ample bundle separate second jets at one point and first jets at two points. For d>=4, a general pencil of degree-three curves is united with a fixed general ordinary degree-(d-3) curve. Incidence calculations separate simple tangency from the Hasse divisor and avoid all loci of codimension at least two. The exact structure-sheaf sequence computes the stable rank of each union and gives loss exactly one at every nonordinary parameter. An ordinary leading coefficient forces this pencil to meet every component of the full Hasse divisor. Thus every generic divisorial inertia group is generated by transvections, and purity gives transvection generation of geometric monodromy in every degree.

A separate argument classifies all invariant covectors on every characteristic-two product of ordinary elliptic curves, without the former parity restriction. Exact Cartier lifts are orthogonal idempotents on global sections. Perfectness of the Cartier pairing and global generation descend these to endomorphisms of F_*L^d. Odd-degree elliptic factors have stable rank-two Frobenius pushforward with scalar endomorphisms; each even-degree factor splits into two distinct character lines. Consequently the full invariant covector dimension is2^e-1, where e counts the even degrees among the factors. On the fixed surface it is0 for odd d and3 for even d. This identifies the entire fixed quotient but does not by itself identify the group on its kernel or all translations. Degrees4 onward remain the fixed-surface full-image gate.

## Checkpoint 07: integral counterexamples and a finite elliptic test

The opening table gives the current status; previous checkpoint sections are dated history. This checkpoint remains INCOMPLETE. It closes all three universal yes/no assertions A.1(a),(b),(c) negatively while retaining their exact-degree obligations.

### A.1(a),(b): explicit integral counterexamples for every prime

For a smooth proper geometrically connected V, write q=h1(O_V), h10=h0(Omega1_V), b=rank_W H1_cris and t=dim H2_cris[p]. Derived crystalline base change in degree0 makes H1_cris free, and degree1 gives h1_dR=b+t. The Hodge and conjugate spectral sequences, without degeneration assumptions, give q-b<=t<=q+h10-b. The exact Cartier and conjugate edge passages in Illusie's1979 original were checked separately from this proof.

Take the previously constructed very ample surface (X,A) with a=h1(A^-1)>0, form Z=X times P1, and embed it with B=A external tensor O(m). For every smooth Y in |B|, the divisor sequence and Kunneth give q(Y)=q(X)+a(m-1). Rational crystalline weak Lefschetz identifies the two first crystalline ranks and makes H2(Z) to H2(Y) rationally injective. Therefore t(Y)-t(Z)>=a(m-1)-h10(X). Choose m so that this is positive. The torsion group on Y then needs strictly more generators than the source torsion, so restriction is not surjective on torsion. Rational injectivity identifies im(u) intersect T(Y) with u(T(Z)); the resulting nonzero torsion quotient embeds in coker(u). Both asserted integral conclusions fail.

The construction is numerical, not conditional on an unknown dimension. In characteristic2 the tame cubic-cover surface has at most35 global one-forms, and the556 ninth-root covers give h10(X)<=591*9^556. Thus m=591*9^556+2 suffices. The fixed X is embedded in P561; the Segre–Veronese product gives the displayed threefold embedding. Independent generic coefficients specify the smooth section over a specified algebraically closed field.

For every prime, put ell=p+1, q0=ell², n0=2 at p2 and n0=p(p-2) otherwise, and g=1+p*ell*n0/2. The first tame cover has at most Bp=g+p+2+p³(p+1)n0/2 global one-forms. The theorem gives an exact finite sum r=h0(L^q0) by Riemann–Roch on the explicitly filtered eigensheaves. The r simultaneous q0th-root covers give h10(X)<=q0^r(Bp+r), so m=q0^r(Bp+r)+2 produces the integral counterexample. The all-prime formulas and local differential inclusions were independently audited, and the arithmetic verifier was independently run for p2,3,5,7,11,13,17,19.

These results prove degree-one failure. They do not yet determine every intermediate integral degree below the existing explicit regularity bound. In particular, coherent equality in degrees>=2 for the characteristic-two surface does not imply integral saturation. Even an isomorphism on p-torsion subgroups would not suffice: multiplication by p maps W/p injectively into W/p² and is an isomorphism on those subgroups.

### A.4: a conditional uniform group theorem with one geometric hypothesis

On the fixed characteristic-two product surface, the full Hasse polynomial is geometrically reduced in every degree. At the generic point of any component, the already proved stable rank drop one gives a right kernel u and left kernel ell with ell(u^[2]) nonzero. Symmetry of Cartier multiplication and beta(u,u)=u^[2] make the derivative of the determinant in direction u nonzero. Repeated factors on a test pencil therefore represent tangencies; they do not imply repeated global components.

The new conditional theorem proves that geometric irreducibility of this divisor would force geometric and arithmetic group GL_(9d²-1)(F2) for every odd d>=3, and the full stabilizer 2^[3(9d²-4)] semidirect GL_(9d²-4)(F2) for every even d>=4. Its proof is complete under that explicit hypothesis, and the hypothesis remains unproved. A saturated invariant subsheaf of the cotangent bundle must receive the entire reduced irreducible determinant divisor: a zero divisor would give a Frobenius-periodic reflexive sheaf, which is locally free by Fitting ideals and trivial by a Lang frame cover, contradicting H0(Omega_P)=0. Every proper invariant quotient is consequently constant, making the common kernel of all known covectors irreducible.

The required large linear subgroup is obtained uniformly from a degree-three curve united with fixed ordinary degree-one curves. Each moving intersection contributes at most S54, and the whole constant-kernel representation is an extension by a2-group, so it cannot share the simple GL80 quotient and its large prime4278255361. Goursat and the independently proved vanishing of H1(GL80,natural or dual) produce an actual natural80 block with trivial complement. This excludes every form-preserving alternative in the checked transvection-group classification. Finally the vanishing of invariant vectors forces the entire translation subgroup in even degree. The root audited each step, including the potentially nonsplit constant-kernel filtration; a quotient surjection alone is not used as a subgroup.

The remaining geometric question is the irreducibility of this particular reduced Hasse divisor. Neither generic stable rank loss one nor a generic-matrix determinantal estimate proves it. The theorem is recorded as a conditional reduction and is not reported as the actual all-degree image classification.

### B.3: exact finite test, with the first wild output still unknown

For every odd p, absolute ramification e and level n, the new proposition gives a finite polynomial test equivalent to the existence of an additive elliptic curve with full rational p^n torsion. The coefficient ring is a length-q Witt ring with a degree-e Eisenstein uniformizer relation. Universal Weierstrass changes detect minimality at bounded precision. A scaled monic division polynomial F and a scaled quadratic G give all nonzero torsion points. Explicit discriminant bounds control every root separation and Jacobian determinant; the adjugate Hensel argument proves that the specified approximate roots lift to the exact required torsion.

Effective elimination over algebraically closed characteristic-p fields decides the finite formula. Its coefficients lie in Fp, so an affirmative result gives a solution over a finite residue field and hence a finite extension of Qp. The proof distinguishes arbitrary perfect residue fields from Fpbar and uses transfer of this finite formula, rather than identifying the two fields. The division-polynomial discriminant constant is defined by an exact rational computation at y²=x³-x; no unproved general closed formula is assumed.

The proof works with the relative de Rham kernel K^j=Omega_Z^j(logY)(-Y). Kunneth kills total degree one in every degree. ### B.3: sharp wild existence at every elliptic level

At p3, let e be positive and even, s=v3(e/2), and m one of3,6,12. Additive elliptic curves with full rational3^n-torsion and exact semistability defect m exist precisely when

    s >= n-1 and e >= (8/m)·3^max(2n-2,4n-4-s).

The construction has algebraically closed perfect residue field and exactly the specified absolute ramification index. If e=2u and L=floor(log3u), the largest additive level is

    N(e) = 1 + min(s, floor((L+1)/2), floor((L+s+1)/4)).

Consequently the least level forcing good reduction among potentially good elliptic curves is N(e)+1. In particular e216 admits full27-torsion with defect12. Combined with the previously proved tame criterion, this determines the elliptic existence question for every odd prime. The higher-dimensional sharp threshold is still open.

The proof constructs a stable arc in the regular universal Drinfeld-level deformation ring of Y²=X³+uX²-X. The odd parameter T=X^-1/2 has [3]_T3=4u. Full-level factorization puts u in the6·3^(2n-2) power of the maximal ideal. A weighted Cohen chart has x=pi^A Z, y=pi and3=pi^d V. Exact traces and norms under the order-three framing action give invariants I and U_y; imposing I=lambda U_y^(A/3) has simple residual equation1-lambda Z². The implicit-function argument therefore gives a complete DVR with the required ramification, preserving the marked full torsion and the faithful wild action. Appropriate congruences on A extend it to groups of orders6 or12. Descent concerns the generic marked elliptic curve; the good integral model is used for its faithful special-fibre inertia action. No averaging by3 or integral descent through a ramified quotient is assumed.

A separate complete obstruction proves that the earlier e24 full9 examples cannot reach e216 full27 merely by a ramification-nine base change. It computes ramification breaks80 and1052 and a norm-uniformizer contradiction. This explains why the new direct equivariant construction is necessary for that route. ### A.4: eventual geometric and arithmetic groups for every ordinary elliptic pair

Let E1 and E2 be any ordinary elliptic curves over a perfect field of characteristic two, let X=E1×E2, and use the product of their cubic polarizations. There is a terminating procedure producing an integer d1 such that, in every odd degree d>=d1, both monodromy groups are GL_(9d²-1)(F2). In every even degree d>=d1, both are the full pointwise stabilizer of the three independent invariant covectors. Writing r=9d²-1, this consists of all matrices [A B;0 I3], with A in GL_(r-3)(F2) and arbitrary B. The representation is irreducible exactly in odd degree. The determinant character is trivial because the coefficient field is F2. This extends the previously completed fixed-product theorem to every ordinary elliptic pair.

The threshold is explicit through a finite terminating search. Enumerate chains of 3-isogenies E_i'→E_i until the product of the source curves has full degree-one GL8 monodromy, as decided by the already proved exact finite frame-cover algorithm. If the resulting odd degrees are m1,m2, take d1=max(m1,m2)+1. This is a terminating algorithm on an effective field of definition, and an existence construction over an arbitrary perfect field; no closed numerical bound uniform in the two elliptic curves is asserted. Chai(1995), Theorem2, printedp477, proves density of the fixed-prime ordinary Hecke orbit over every algebraically closed field. The checked geometric-integrality spreading lemma, Stacks Tag0559, Lemma37.27.5, supplies the nonempty open set of full-GL8 pairs that this search must meet.

There are two geometric steps. First, a universal one-factor Cartier calculation on y²+xy=x³+A x²+B, B nonzero, gives a constant invertible symmetrizer. Tensoring it gives a symmetric nine-dimensional pencil whose diagonal entries are independent coordinates. Its determinant contains the full squarefree diagonal monomial with coefficient one; every nonidentity matching term has a squared factor. This proves that the degree-one Hasse divisor is geometrically reduced. Uniform two-point jet bounds and a mixed-tacnode argument then show that the Hasse divisor is geometrically irreducible and reduced in every degree d>=2, with generic stable-rank drop exactly one. The common kernel of all invariant covectors is irreducible in every such degree. These assertions do not require finding the isogeny threshold.

Second, a generic degree-one curve on E1'×E2' has an ordinary nodal image under the odd product isogeny. The proof verifies double-point transversality and excludes triple points, including the loci where one translation coordinate is zero. The image has the exact divisor class O(3m2O) external tensor O(3m1O); the norm calculation controls the Picard-zero part as well. Adding a fixed grid realizes a degeneration in every degree d>=d1. The degree-one GL8 quotient can be isolated as an actual block acting trivially on a complement: the remaining node covers have symmetric-group factors of degree at most18, and their groups have no prime31, whereas GL8 has such a factor. The previously checked Goursat and first-cohomology arguments split the block. Transvection-group classification and the full translation-subgroup calculation then give the stated groups. All three invariant covectors in even degree are defined over the original perfect field, so the arithmetic group is the same pointwise stabilizer.

The root and reduction-descent agent each read and checked both complete proofs. The primary sources above were opened at the actual statements. The exact symmetrizer and product-isogeny incidence programs were independently executed; their finite calculations certify the algebraic identities and listed configurations, while the nodal and group arguments are proved in the paper.

### A.3: an exact all-degree parity formula on a supersingular product

For E0:y²+y=x³ over F2, X=E0×E0 and the same cubic product polarization, the generic relative stable rank is

    9d²-1 if d is odd; 9d²-3 if d is even.

The coherent relative dimension is 9d²-1, so generic relative Frobenius is bijective exactly in odd degree. Every even degree gives a defect of two. The ambient Picard scheme is smooth and crystalline cohomology is torsion-free. Thus this persistent defect has a different source from the earlier torsion counterexample. The full generic curve has genus9d²+1 and p-rank defect two or four according to parity. Its generic relative stable rank is positive in every positive degree.

For the upper bounds, restriction embeds Pic0(X) as an actual abelian subvariety of the curve Jacobian. The proof uses negative-twist Kunneth vanishing both to exclude a nontrivial geometric kernel and to show that the tangent kernel is zero. The dual norm map q has smooth connected kernel P, and its composite with the ambient inclusion is multiplication by m=3d. Thus f_Y=f_P and dimP=g(Y)-2. If m is even, X[2] embeds into P[2]. Functorial Verschiebung on these finite group schemes embeds the connected order-four kernel of V_X into the connected part of kerV_P. This forces dimP-f_P>=2 and the stronger even-degree upper bound. The argument concerns finite group schemes, not just their geometric points.

For the lower bounds, explicit Q8 automorphisms of E0 realize every positive integer as the degree of an endomorphism. The paper includes a complete four-square descent, including its equality case, and verifies the quaternion identities directly. For odd m, choose b with b†b=bb†=[m-1]. The isogeny [1 b;-b† 1] pulls back the product principal divisor to two supersingular elliptic graphs meeting transversely in m² points. The resulting nodal divisor has precisely the required line bundle N_m: equality of polarization homomorphisms gives a Pic0 difference, symmetry makes it 2-torsion, and the supersingular product has no nontrivial geometric 2-torsion points. Its stable rank is m²-1. Constant cohomology dimensions over the complete linear system, the finite-iterate rank condition and Bertini transfer that lower bound to generic smooth members. For even m=3d, a transverse union of sharp odd members in degrees3 andm-3 has stable rankm²-3. The same deformation argument gives equality. Projective normality identifies these complete linear systems with the actual ambient hypersurface systems.

The root and monodromy agent independently audited the complete proof. A separate exact certificate verifies the explicit automorphisms, both symbolic quaternion norm identities,4096 four-square witnesses, the degeneration arithmetic through10000 degrees, and all six retained Cartier matrix witnesses. The uniform theorem is proved geometrically and does not rely on extrapolating the finite checks.

### A.4: full degree-one monodromy despite a double Hasse component

The same supersingular product has geometric and arithmetic degree-one image GL8(F2) over every perfect characteristic-two base. In tensor coefficient coordinates s with3×3 matrixS, its relative Hasse polynomial is

    h(s)=det(S)²·(s^[2]t (J3 tensor J3) s),

where J3 reverses the three basis coordinates. The determinantal cubic and the second, smooth cubic are geometrically irreducible and distinct. Thus a double Hasse component is compatible with full geometric monodromy.

The proof derives the Cartier pencil from C(1)=0, C(x)=1, C(y)=x, C(xy)=y and the zero values on squares. After reversing the tensor basis it is the Hessian of detS. Its nine maximal Pfaffians are det(S) times the coefficient vector. The adjugate formula gives the determinant on the relative Euler-sequence hyperplane exactly, including both factors and their multiplicities.

Two explicit F8 points complete the group argument. The first has relative Frobenius polynomial T8+T7+T5+T+1, irreducible and nonreciprocal; an independent restriction-of-scalars27×27 characteristic-polynomial calculation confirms it. The second lies on the simple cubic component and has relative stable rank seven. Along an explicit tangent direction the determinant has a simple zero, and the first derivative on the one-dimensional Frobenius kernel has a nonzero component in the seven-dimensional stable subspace. A complete local lemma turns this into a wild transvection: lift the seven unit directions over a strict trait, write the last Frobenius column as (b(t),a(t)) with a of order one, and solve the Lang lift by Artin–Schreier equations with right side b/a². A nonzero simple pole forces ramification and hence a rank-one unipotent inertia element. Its normal closure, together with the irreducible nonreciprocal Frobenius element, is GL8 by the already checked transvection classification. The dense smooth-open restriction preserves the image; the two coefficient certificates do not require an additional claim that those particular curves are smooth.

The root and reduction-descent agent independently checked the complete proof and reran both the symbolic and extension-field certificates. The proof explicitly handles the Frobenius convention and the normal-closure step; it does not infer geometric monodromy solely from arithmetic Frobenius data.

### B.4: stronger wild lower bounds and an explicit 9-torsion ambiguity

The every-level wild elliptic construction now gives the character lower bound

    ν_min(3,2u,h) >= 1+min(s, floor((L+1)/2), floor((L+s+1)/4)),

The two groups have identical full truncation at the indicated power of3, while the finite inertia action on one contains the order-four automorphism whose square is[-1]. This improves the earlier general lower bounds. It does not determine the least index.

A different explicit construction improves the smallest odd-ramification case further. Over K=Q3(zeta3), take

    B: y²-xy+y=x³-x²-28x-53,
    N=T_q[3∞], q=23328,
    M=B[3∞].

Then B[9] is isomorphic to T_q[9], whereas the manuscript characters have values-2 and2 on an inertia element of order two in the finite inertia image. Consequently ν_min(3,2,h)>=2 for every h>=2. The same construction proves the lower bound for every e congruent2 modulo4. The index convention is M(ν)=ker3^(ν+1); equality at9 is equality of M(1).

All data are explicit. The source curve y²-3xy-12y=x³-12x² has the rational point P=(0,0) of exact order nine. The paper displays all its multiples and an exact degree-nine rational isogeny with kernel generated byP, followed by the rational change of variables giving B. The descent function

    f=(x²-y)³(y-3x+4)/(x-4)³

has divisor9[P]-9[O]. Evaluating at5P and4P gives the connecting class-32/27; multiplying by(-3)^9 gives q=23328. The dual-isogeny sequence is the extension of Z/9 by μ9 classified by that Kummer class, and Tate's explicit finite group scheme identifies it with T_q[9]. The exact invariants c4=17·3^4 andΔ=-2·3^9 give integralj and potential good reduction forB. Its raw discriminant valuation overK is18, so every good-reduction ramification degreem satisfies12 dividing18m and hence is even. The faithful special-fibre inertia image therefore contains[-1], whose Dieudonne trace is-2. The Tate group satisfies the full manuscript filtration condition with good graded pieces μ_(3∞) andQ3/Z3, giving character2. It need not itself acquire good reduction, and the proof uses precisely the broader condition in B.4.

The root independently audited the whole construction and read Antieau–Auel(2021), AppendixA.1, LemmasA.1–A.2, printedp22, including the normalization discussion, and Tate(1995), “Points of finite order,” pp18–19. The retained certificate checks all81 subgroup additions, the degree-nine rational identity, the target change, both sets of invariants, the divisor identities and the Kummer adjustment. Whether index2 already suffices for every height-two group at(p,e)=(3,2) remains open.

# Root research and source log, checkpoint09 interval

## Completed extension to every ordinary elliptic pair

The root read both ordinary_products_general_structure.tex and ordinary_products_isogeny_full_groups.tex completely. The first proof passes: the actual root-removed elliptic Cartier matrix has an invertible constant symmetrizer R, although R itself need not be symmetric. Its tensor square has independent diagonal coordinates. The symmetric determinant expansion in characteristic two proves geometric squarefreeness from the coefficient of the squarefree full diagonal monomial. The product jet ranks and their exceptional loci remain valid for every ordinary cubic pair; the independent polar quadratics rule out a strange cubic. The degree-one Hasse components have smooth generic curves of Cartier corank one. The mixed-tacnode proof then supplies irreducibility, reducedness and generic stable loss one in every degree at least two. The all-parity invariant-covector proof already applies to distinct factors, so the common kernel is irreducible and there are no invariant vectors.

The second proof also passes. Connectedness of the full degree-one frame cover at the fixed F2 pair forces geometric generic connectedness: after finite normalization of the excellent coefficient base, distinct components of a normal finite etale cover would be disjoint and each would surject onto the integral base, contradicting the connected special fibre. Smoothness then makes that generic fibre geometrically irreducible, and Stacks Lemma37.27.5(Tag0559) gives a nonempty open of full GL8 images. Under an odd product isogeny, the generic degree-one curve has an integral ordinary nodal image. The two-point rank-five exceptions and triple Segre collinearity incidence have the stated dimensions. Norm of the divisor gives the exact line bundle, with no undetermined Picard twist. Each node cover has degree18 and each added-grid intersection splits into degree3 covers. The graph image and its constant extension have prime factors at most17. Simplicity of GL8 and prime31, followed by the natural-module H1 splitting, give an actual GL8 block with trivial complement. This excludes every form-preserving transvection alternative and supplies the full translation subgroup in even degree.

Chai's original published paper was opened through its indexed full text after the direct author-PDF request returned403. The root read Theorem2, printedp477, and its definition of the fixed-prime Hecke orbit in the introduction. It states density of every fixed ell-power orbit of an ordinary point over every algebraically closed field, and therefore applies withg1,ell3. The root also read the full statement and both proofs of Stacks0559. The product of the two dense orbits meets the full-image open. Enumerating finite chains of3-isogenies and applying the already proved exact frame algorithm therefore terminates. The resulting isogeny degrees m1,m2 give the effective threshold max(m1,m2)+1. Arithmetic covectors descend over the original perfect field because the unique nontrivial2-character on each factor and the normalized differential dx/x are defined there. This proves both entire groups for every larger degree. It does not claim that every arbitrary pair has full degree-one image, nor classify arbitrary ambient varieties.

The root read and independently executed both exact programs. ordinary_product_universal_symmetrizer.py returned PASS in0.48850249600218376s, checking the universal polynomial matrices and all nine tensor coefficients. ordinary_product_isogeny_incidence_checks.py returned PASS in0.0528557190009451s:18424 triples among49 rank-one F2 matrices,98 collinear triples, the common-factor criterion, all three parallel-gradient relations and the prime31 bound. The original author outputs were preserved. Root outputs are outputs/root_ordinary_product_universal_symmetrizer.json and outputs/root_ordinary_product_isogeny_incidence_checks.json. Finite checks support universal proofs rather than replacing them. The reduction-descent agent independently passed both complete proofs and reopened the two primary sources. Both files were authorized for promotion after these audits.

## B.4: a strictly stronger wild lower bound

The root read B_character_wild_lower.tex in full. B28 passes: the newly constructed defect12 elliptic curve with full3^N torsion, plus h-2 constant etale factors, and the constant height-h group have isomorphic3^N truncations. An order-four inertia element has traces h-2 and h because its elliptic eigenvalues are i,-i. The manuscript truncation is M(nu)=M[p^(nu+1)], so failure at3^N gives least universal index at leastN. This is the wild maximum already checked in25000 exact integer comparisons. It improves the tame lower bound, for example to2 at e24 and3 at e216, in every height at least two. It is a lower bound, not a claimed exact least index for arbitrary pairs.

## Research searches and unproved directions

The root queried both engines for generic p-rank of ample curves on a fixed surface, Cartier nilpotent forms, generic ordinarity of arbitrary hypersurface sections, and generic Cartier rank on an abelian surface. Most results concerned full moduli, special covers, or normalization formulas. The 2017 paper Lefschetz theorems for torsion algebraic cycles in codimension2 (DOI10.1016/J.AIM.2017.05.018) appeared with a conditional Illusie statement requiring ordinary linear complete intersections on the ambient variety. A search-snippet sentence about generic complete intersections was not imported as an arbitrary fixed-ambient theorem. The primary theorem has not been used from that snippet. The Koblitz original and previously audited Illusie results remain distinguished from the unresolved structural generic-rank formula.

Two further approaches remain exploratory. One is a theta-group symmetrizer on an arbitrary ordinary principally polarized abelian surface in characteristic two, using the level-three Heisenberg representation; its required jet-rank strata have not yet been proved. The other transports a full GL80 seed through a general odd isogeny. It requires control of self-tacnode and triple-point boundary divisors and of the full node permutation images; the product-isogeny argument alone does not prove those statements. Neither approach is promoted or counted as a result.

## New proved reductions

For the fixed supersingular product E0×E0 in characteristic two, the complete invariant-covector space is now determined in every degree: dimension0 in odd degree and1 in even degree, for both geometric and arithmetic monodromy. The even covector is the explicit double invariant derivative of the defining section. A separate complete proof places an actual naturalGL8(F2) block with trivial complement in every degree. This excludes invariant nondegenerate alternating forms on the full representation and on the even common kernel. Full higher-degree images and irreducibility remain unresolved; neither statement is promoted beyond its scope.

One fixed mixed surface is now settled in all degrees over the perfect fieldF2(t)^perf: Et:y²+txy+y=x³ times E0:y²+y=x³, embedded by the cubic product polarization. Its relative ranks are9d²−1 in odd degree and9d²−2 in even degree. The same proof applies to every transcendental ordinaryj; it does not include all algebraicj. The proof uses specialization of a relative coherent vector bundle at the supersingular fibre and a connected-Prym upper bound. The same generic elliptic factor is used in every degree.

The characteristic-two theta-surface prerequisites have complete proofs: a direct genus-two second-jet construction at every point; all two-first-jet rank strata and their dimensions; finite exceptional second points for ordinary surfaces; three distinct value separation; an irreducible discriminant with generic integral one-node ordinary curve; and ordinary generic members in every degree. The elementary product-versus-Jacobian dichotomy covers every principally polarized surface over the algebraic closure. These proofs do not import a characteristic-zero jet theorem.

The level-three theta-group calculation gives a symmetric Cartier matrix in every dimension, with diagonal equal to the coefficient vector after ordinary Cartier normalization. Its determinant is geometrically squarefree. The Frobenius-twisted target is typed explicitly and the dual identification uses only the finite centralmu3. This supplies the degree-one Hasse input for the broader ordinary-ppav image theorem. The combined theorem has now passed three complete proof audits (root, obstruction/crystal, reduction/descent) and is included as proved. For every ordinary principally polarized abelian surface in characteristic2, in the3Theta embedding, the eventual geometric image is GL_(9d²−1)(F2) in odd degree and the full pointwise stabilizer of three independent covectors in even degree. The entire arithmetic even image is the preimage of the actual Galois permutation subgroupGamma ofS3 on those three characters. The terminating polarized3-isogeny search supplies multipliern, and the explicit ambient threshold ismax(n+1,reg(I_A)−1). The auxiliary isogeny is allowed over a finite extension; the arithmetic conclusion remains over the original perfect field. Hasse and common-kernel irreducibility hold fromd2 in the complete systems. No arbitrary-surface or characteristic-odd extension is claimed.

B.4 now has a sharper universal finite upper bound from Bondarko's exact exponent, and the small casep3,e2,h2 satisfies2<=nu_min<=4 in manuscript indexingM(nu)=M[3^(nu+1)]. A27-torsion comparison already separates a potentially supersingular group from a height-one-filtered group. Any possible81-torsion character failure must involve two potentially supersingular groups with faithful inertia orders12 versus3 or6, and joint faithful image of order36. This is a strict restriction on the remaining tests, not an existence or exact-index claim.

## Supersingular invariant covectors and actual linear blocks

The root read supersingular_exact_covectors.tex completely and independently rederived every step. The linearized image already has the stable rank in both parities. An arbitrary invariant covector lifts uniquely after one Frobenius-root extension; its square is rational, has no divisorial poles, and is a global quadratic section. The common kernel of all Cartier coefficients is zero by the perfect Cartier pairing and global generation of the Frobenius pushforward. Comparing monomials of odd exponent kills all quadratic cross terms. The resulting linear operator descends to an endomorphism of F_*N. Odd-degree simplicity and the even-degree nonsplit unipotent algebra leave exactly0 and1 relative invariant covectors, respectively. The unique even covector is defined overF2, so geometric and arithmetic invariant spaces agree. This is a complete covector calculation, not a full image classification.

The root also read supersingular_uniform_GL8_block.tex completely. In odd degree the quaternion norm supplies a matching odd self-isogeny; product jet and triple-value incidences give generic nodal images in the exact desired line bundle. In even degree the intersection cover with a fixed sharp odd member has full symmetric group, using double transitivity and a section with exactly one double zero. Its entire graph-plus-constant image has no GL8 composition factor, not merely a bounded degree. Goursat and the proved natural-dual H1 vanishing extract an actual natural GL8 block with trivial complement in both parities. This excludes invariant nondegenerate alternating forms; the full higher-degree irreducibility/image question remains open.

Root symbolic rerun supersingular_cartier_operator_checks.py passed in0.06592429800002719s. It verifies the four universal local operator identities and the bounded symmetrizer dimensions1,0,0,0,0,0 in one-factor poledegrees3,5,7,9,11,13. No extrapolation is made from that finite list. Root output is outputs/root_supersingular_cartier_operator_checks.json, with author output preserved.

## Theta jets, their source scope, and the next monodromy extension

Both search engines were used for three-theta jet separation, two-point first jets, characteristic-two Kummer separation, and higher embeddings of abelian surfaces. Opened Bauer–Szemberg1997, Higher order embeddings of abelian varieties, authorPDF, Theorems1/2 and2.1. Its conventions explicitly work overC (PDFp2); it is not imported in characteristic two. Opened Alvarado–Pareschi, arXiv2502.11288v4 dated8June2026, TheoremD and its proof, with the explicit characteristic-zero convention inSection2. Its stronger general jet theorem is likewise not imported. These access limits are recorded in the bibliography. A characteristic-two Kummer article by Dolgachev appeared in search, but no theorem from it was used: elementary products Theta_a+Theta_-a prove the exact two-theta point separation needed here.

The root wrote a complete direct proof in sections/A_ppav_two_jets.tex. It includes an elementary all-characteristic dichotomy for principally polarized abelian surfaces; exact second-jet surjectivity for a smooth genus-two theta divisor; two-first-jet ranks6 off a dimension-two locus, at least5 off a dimension-one81-curve locus, and at least4 everywhere; finite exceptional second points for each fixed first point on an ordinary surface; and an irreducible discriminant with generic integral one-node member. A three-theta product separates any three distinct values in the smooth-theta case. A generic sum of three theta translates with parameter sum zero gives an ordinary nodal member, hence ordinary generic members in every positive degree. The obstruction/crystal agent independently audited all proofs, including the later dichotomy, ordinary-member and three-value additions.

The root read ppav_theta_cartier_symmetrizer.tex and independently checked its fully typed map beta:W tensor W -> W^(2). On the finite level-three Heisenberg subgroup, rho(h)^[2]=rho(h)^(-transpose). Modulation forces a+b+c=0, simultaneous translation realizes cyclic permutation, and symmetry in the first two entries therefore gives full symmetry. The diagonal is c*s, withc1 after ordinary Cartier normalization. This proves a squarefree degree3^g determinant, in particular a geometrically reduced degree-one Hasse divisor for any ordinary principally polarized surface. The whole centralGm is not identified with its dual. Root read the primary AV draft Corollary8.20p119, Lemma8.24p121, Theorem8.28 and description8.29pp122–123, and Corollary9.17pp133–134, including the proofs, at https://van-der-geer.nl/~gerard/AV.pdf . ## A fixed mixed surface over a perfect function field

The root read A3_mixed_generic_ranks.tex and its code completely. The proof passes: in the smooth elliptic family y²+txy+y=x³, the relative coherent quotient is a vector bundle of constant rank across the supersingular fibre t0. For each odd degree its invertibility there forces invertibility at the same generic t; no countable intersection of opens or new choice of the elliptic factor is made. In even degree the connected Verschiebung-kernel inclusion in the Prym supplies the upper bound and a union of two sharp odd members supplies the lower bound. Thus the fixed surface overF2(t)^perf has relative ranks9d²−1 odd and9d²−2 even. The same holds for every transcendental ordinary j. The arbitrary algebraic ordinary-factor question remains open; bounded explicitF2 probes are not promoted to an all-degree proof.

## Completed ordinary-ppav structural extension,10:14UTC

The combined ordinary_ppav_surface_full_groups.tex has now passed three independent complete proof audits: root, obstruction/crystal, and reduction/descent. Every ordinary principally polarized abelian surface in characteristic two is included after the stated terminating threshold, not just products. The exact invariant quotient is0odd/3even; the common kernel is irreducible fromd2 and the Hasse divisor is irreducible/reduced with generic stable loss one. A full degree-one open in moduli follows from the connected frame cover of the certified product seed and normality after a finite base normalization. A polarized odd isogeny from that open gives a generic nodal image, with exact norm line bundle corrected by a fixed geometric two-torsion translation. The fixed added divisor has pullback an integral ample curve, whose intersection permutation group is the full symmetric group. The entire graph-plus-constant image has no GL8 composition factor, and Goursat with the proved H1 vanishing supplies an actual block. Transvection classification, common-kernel irreducibility and absence of invariant vectors yield the entire groups.

The auxiliary isogeny is explicitly permitted over a finite extension of the original perfect field. Arithmetic descent is kept over the original field: in even degree its entire image is the preimage of the Galois permutation subgroupGamma on the three nonzero Frobenius-kernel characters. The ambient threshold adds reg(I_A)−1, so no characteristic-two projective-normality theorem is silently assumed. The threshold is a terminating algorithm over effective fields of definition, not a numerical bound independent of the surface.

Root reopened Chai1995's indexed original Theorem2 p477 for the full arbitrary-dimension/fixed-prime statement (turn576289185search0); the direct author PDF still returned403. It is accurately cited as the opened exposition; the original GIT/compactification proof was not obtained anew. The ordinary open and an etale-local nonsymmetric representative of the polarization suffice, avoiding an inseparable symmetric-theta torsor in characteristic two.

## Checkpoint 11: universal surface ranks and nonordinary invariant quotients

## Current state

This checkpoint remains **INCOMPLETE**. The open main rows remain A.3, A.4, B.3 in higher dimension, and B.4. No arbitrary-ambient classification is inferred from the new abelian-surface theorems. This note records only fully audited mathematics; pending further candidates are listed separately.

## Exact rank formula for all principal surfaces

For every principally polarized abelian surface (A,Theta) in characteristic two, let L=3Theta and let f_A be its geometric2-rank. In the complete linear system |L^d|, every positive degree has generic relative stable rank9d²−1 when d is odd and9d²−3+f_A when d is even. The coherent relative dimension is9d²−1. Thus Frobenius is generically bijective precisely in odd degree or when A is ordinary. This closes the previously explicit algebraic mixed-factor refinement, including every ordinary j-invariant. For the fixed ambient embedding, surjective restriction is guaranteed at d≥max(1,reg(I_A)−1); products have surjective restriction in every degree. The entire proof, including the exceptional Pfaffian tensors, the relative determinant, odd norm construction and even connected-Prym bound, passed root and independent monodromy audits.

## Entire invariant quotients on nonordinary principal surfaces

For every nonordinary principally polarized abelian surface in characteristic two, the complete geometric and arithmetic invariant-covector spaces have dimension0 in every odd degree and2^{f_A} in every even degree. In particular the two-rank-zero cases both have one covector, including the nonzero nilpotent chain, and the two-rank-one cases have two. The arithmetic action on these invariant spaces is trivial over the original perfect field. The proof handles an arbitrary persistent nilpotent length before reducing to the entire four-dimensional distribution algebra of the Frobenius kernel. Both other Part-A agents independently audited the complete proof, its relative-Frobenius conventions, the local identities and arithmetic descent. The whole group in these general nonordinary families remains open.

## Two further exact supersingular groups

On the fixed product E0×E0, E0:y²+y=x³ overF2, with the cubic Segre embedding, the degree-two rank is33 and both complete images areF2^{32} semidirect GL32(F2), the full pointwise stabilizer of the unique covector. The proof uses one irreducible nonreciprocal degree32 Frobenius polynomial with primitive prime65537, and a second literal matrix witness showing that an arithmetic Frobenius has no fixed lift of the invariant quotient. The already proved actual geometricGL8 block and the self-normalizing-complement argument force the full geometric group.

The degree-three geometric and arithmetic groups are bothGL80(F2). Its retained Frobenius certificate has an irreducible nonreciprocal degree80 polynomial with primitive prime4278255361. Together with the same actual geometric block this gives the entire group. These two proofs passed independent root and reduction/descent audits. Exact full groups in degrees one through three are settled; no conclusion for every higher degree is inferred from the finite searches.

## The height-two comparison graph

In the remaining norm-one, potentially supersingular height-two comparison with different tame special-inertia orders, a level3^n isomorphism forces the special graph at every smaller level r to be ker(F_M^r)×ker(F_N^r). Its rank-nine successive finite-flat quotients have special fibre alpha3²; multiplication by3 is generically an isomorphism and specially zero. This is proved using integral pairings on full Barsotti–Tate groups, flat graph orthogonality and tame projectors. It is not by itself counted as a new numerical exclusion of a least index.

## Further promotions after the actual hourly review

The full rank theorem now extends to every abelian surface in characteristic two with ampleM of odd Euler characteristicD=chi(M), in the complete linear systems of L=M³. The exact ranks are9Dd²−1 in odd degree and9Dd²−3+f_A in even degree. The integral dual polarization produces a finite étale principal source of degreeD, and its norm construction supplies the sharp odd-degree rank. The even-degree obstruction uses the dual polarizationmD lambda_M^{-1} on the connected Prym. Root and monodromy independently checked the entire proof and certificate. Even-degree polarizations and arbitrary embeddings outside the stated third-power/regularity scope remain unresolved.

The sharpened height-two logarithm estimate is also proved after complete root and obstruction/crystal proof, primary-source and certificate audits. For a target whose special fibre is a product of height-two one-dimensional formal groups, the coefficient valuations followq0=1 andq(i+1)=min(a+q(i),1+3q(i),9q(i)). The completed Cartier-module argument kills the special kernel byFrob^u as soon asq(u)>a/2. At absolute ramification72, q3=40>36; combined with the exact graph this excludes every81-torsion ambiguity. Consequently2≤nu_min(3,2,2)≤3. A27-torsion failure, if it exists, has joint good-reduction image of order18 or36. No such example or a decision between indices2 and3 is asserted.
