# Root source and proof log after checkpoint10

Credit: GPT-6 Astra (OpenAI, ChatGPT Pro mode). No priority claim.

## Universal nonordinary covectors

Searches actually issued on19 November2026:

- `abelian variety restricted Lie algebra p-map Hasse Witt Frobenius Cartier p-rank`.
- `abelian variety Frobenius pushforward endomorphisms restricted enveloping algebra kernel Frobenius`.
- `"Hasse-Witt" "abelian surface" monodromy covectors`.
- `"Frobenius" "invariant covectors" abelian`.

The routine engine returned teaching notes, Frobenius-splitting papers and p-rank discussions. The second, stronger search returned Shimura-surface Hasse-Witt work and unrelated covector uses. No retrieved result supplied the asserted entire invariant-covector classification. This is an access/search report, not a claim of novelty or priority.

The root reopened the complete Edixhoven–van der Geer–Moonen AV draft at https://van-der-geer.nl/~gerard/AV.pdf. It read Proposition4.45 with proof pp67–68; Proposition5.22 and its proof pp80–81; Theorem7.5 with proof p100; and Proposition7.34 with full proof p111. The last proof identifies the dual of relative Frobenius with Verschiebung by pulling back rigidified line bundles and obtaining their p-th tensor powers. Combined with finite-isogeny duality, this identifies the Cartier dual of the entire Frobenius kernel with kerV on the dual abelian variety. The direct torsor/adjunction proof in the new section then computes the endomorphism algebra without invoking an unproved splitting of F_*O. The proof of odd-degree simplicity uses the original Oda1971 Theorem1.2(i), p45, with its proof pp43–45, already read in checkpoint10. The relative Frobenius pairing uses the previously opened Brion–Kumar Lemma1.3.6, Proposition1.3.7 and Theorem1.3.8, pp26–28; the obstruction/crystal agent independently reopened and read those proofs again.

The new nil-length lemma was written and proved in full: a covector on the stable image is first extended over a purely inseparable root field; its q-th power has no divisorial poles and is a homogeneous polynomial of degree q. A coefficientwise induction on two-adic divisibility removes every nonpure monomial, using the common kernel of the linear matrix coefficients. This proves a linear lift for every nilpotent length, not only the first-image-stable case from the supersingular product. The two independent Part-A agents checked this entire argument, the rational normal forms for all restricted-Lie types, the exact semilinear Cartier table and arithmetic descent. The root certificate took0.09968187000049511 seconds; the independent reruns took0.09809181400123634 and0.1044188160012709 seconds. Each output remains separate.

## Universal principal-surface rank proof

The root read the full new A3_ppav_surface_ranks.tex and independently rederived its Pfaffian, simplicity, norm and Prym arguments. The coefficient cubic has exactly the forty stated exceptional points; the Fourier and phase transformations supply actual projectors there, contradicting simplicity after fibrewise descent. The relative determinant is explicitly computed, so full linearized rank is not substituted for relative stable rank. The odd-degree isogenies are obtained from maximal isotropic quotients separately for every odd degree; they do not require a self-isogeny of the fixed surface. All exceptional two-jet and triple-value loci in the norm construction were checked, including one-coordinate kernel translations on products. In even degree the connected Prym contains the full scheme A[2], giving the matching defect upper bound.

The search `Debarre 1994 Degrees curves abelian varieties 346 347 pdf` obtained the author's copy and the original Numdam article. The root opened https://www.numdam.org/item/10.24033/bsmf.2236.pdf and read Section3, especially3.1–3.3, printed pp346–347, together with the arbitrary-algebraically-closed-field convention on p346. Equation3.2 identifies the norm endomorphism of a curve numerically equivalent to mTheta on a principal surface with[m]; equation3.3 identifies its induced Jacobian polarization. This verifies the multiplier also when m is even. A screenshot request returned an image marker through the web wrapper; that marker alone was not counted as a visual reading. The root read the original extracted equations and their scope, and the monodromy agent independently viewed the two printed pages.

The complete finite certificate was read and independently rerun by the root in0.29324621799969464 seconds: nine symbolic Pfaffians,945 matchings, all forty actual exceptional projectors, Fourier/phase identities,1700 supplementary determinant values, and30000 dimension identities. The complete output is outputs/root_A3_ppav_rank_checks.json; the author's larger output and original timings were preserved. The monodromy independent full proof/source/code audit also passed.

## Checkpoint 12: coherent crystals and inseparable surface polarizations

## Current state

This checkpoint remains **INCOMPLETE**. Main rows A.3, A.4, B.3 in higher dimension, and B.4 retain explicitly stated open gates. The simultaneous locally Noetherian/coherent refinement of E.2 is now proved. The unrestricted E.2 assertion remains refuted. All assertions promoted below passed a complete independent proof audit; finite arithmetic checks are separately identified and do not substitute for geometric existence proofs.

## Coherent crystals over a Noetherian base

For every flat locally finitely presented morphism X→S with S locally Noetherian, restriction gives an equivalence between ordinary coherent stratified modules and quasi-coherent infinitesimal crystals whose value on X is coherent. The statement holds on both the small and big infinitesimal sites, including arbitrary classical tests, and in every characteristic. Values are coherent on Noetherian tests and finitely presented on arbitrary tests. The coefficient module need not be flat.

The proof establishes a universally exact completed augmented Cech complex by filtering along the nilpotent test ideal. This descends finite Hom modules and proves ordinary full faithfulness. Flatness of the completed formal-nerve projections lifts the coherent stratification to derived complete data. Jiang's precisely checked comparison yields a derived crystal. A finite nilpotent filtration proves that its values are in D≤0; taking H0 then gives ordinary Cartesian pullback. The proof does not claim that the intermediate derived crystal is discrete. Finite-type graph neighbourhoods, common refinements and equalizers extend it to arbitrary tests. The root, monodromy and reduction/descent agents independently checked the entire proof and its original source bridge.

The prior nonfaithfulness examples are unchanged: dropping coherence can fail even over a regular Noetherian base, and dropping the Noetherian condition can fail for a rank-one structure crystal. Their scope remarks now point to the proved narrower theorem, rather than describing it as undecided.

## Every polarization on an ordinary surface

Let A be an ordinary abelian surface in characteristic two, let M be any ample line bundle, put D=chi(M), and use the embedding L=M³. For every positive d the geometric generic smooth curve in |L^d| is ordinary of genus 9Dd²+1, and its relative stable rank is exactly 9Dd²−1. No parity restriction on D or d remains.

The integral dual polarization is constructed by descent along lambda_M. In its two-primary kernel, the connected multiplicative factor is maximal isotropic. Choosing it makes the dual principal-source isogeny finite etale even when its degree is even. A sharp genus-ten ordinary source curve has a nodal norm image; the collision incidence proof is checked for even-order translations. An arbitrary target translation puts the norm in the exact desired linear system. Transverse unions of ordinary members then prove every degree. The existing ambient regularity bound remains explicit; no assertion about an arbitrary embedding being a cube is made.

## Sharp ranks for inseparable supersingular product types

On A=E×E, E:y²+y=x³ in characteristic two, let M=O_E(aO) external O_E(bO), where a,b are positive integers, and put L=M³. If exactly one factor is even and that integer has 2-adic valuation one, the generic relative ranks are 9abd²−2 for odd d and 9abd²−3 for even d. If both a,b are even, the rank is 9abd²−3 for every d. The coherent relative dimension is 9abd²−1. Types with exactly one even factor of higher 2-adic valuation are not covered by this theorem.

The proof first obtains global Cartier-kernel relations from the canonical connection on an even-degree factor. An exact (6,3) base matrix has rank(C)=rank(C²)=16. Odd-degree product isogenies propagate this sharp base, with a complete nodality proof allowing kernel projections that are not injective. Transverse unions of two sharp odd product types give the both-even lower bound. The complete independent audit also reconstructed all eighteen matrix columns from the curve-ring identity f=a²+x b², without importing the author's monomial Cartier formula.

## The supersingular global contact witness

The local self-tacnode and triple-point Cartier lemmas are now proved after full independent audits. One global norm-family witness has also been certified over GF64. Its source is geometrically smooth; its degree-three norm image has exactly one simple self-tacnode and otherwise ordinary nodes. The relative Hasse divisor is smooth at this image. The exact coefficient vector is (38,14,10,47,47,43,30,4,8), in the specified monomial tensor basis. All four smooth-source charts and all144 collision ideals were checked over the algebraic closure, with genuine rational translation identities and nilpotent-support tests. A separate truncated elliptic-addition calculation verifies the simple-contact coefficient55 and mixed Cartier value60.

The first two candidates failed because of unwanted triple collisions; their inputs, outputs and failure transcripts remain in the archive. Two implementation errors in the initial finite-field interface are retained as failed runs as well. No rational-point sampling was used to assert a global exclusion. The candidate finite collision reduction remains explicitly unpromoted: two distinct tangent-triple orbit witnesses and the later uniform propagation step still have to be completed. The known full groups in degrees one through three are unchanged.

## The remaining height-two character test is elliptic

For p=3, absolute ramification e=2 and height h=2, the least manuscript index remains either2 or3; 81-torsion always suffices. Every potentially supersingular norm-one comparison in the remaining case can now be realized by elliptic curves, preserving the integral Tate lattice and the special inertia action. The proof uses explicit integral quaternion conjugacy, labelled Serre–Tate deformation, algebraization and generic Galois descent. It does not descend a good model across ramification.

For a potentially supersingular elliptic curve, the tame special-inertia quotient has order t=4/gcd(4,v(Delta)). Thus the residual level27 ambiguity is equivalent to two triples (a2,a4,a6) over W(k)[pi]/(pi²−3), for an algebraically closed field k of characteristic three, with 0≤v(Delta)≤23, 3v(c4)>v(Delta), isomorphic generic27-torsion, and discriminant valuations in different columns of {0 mod4,2 mod4,odd}. This is a proved six-coefficient criterion. Its existence or nonexistence has not been decided.

## Final checkpoint verification

The final paper has305 pages and2,289,105 bytes. The reproducible final build passed in31.906 seconds with zero overfull boxes and no unresolved references or citations. The initial build had one123.4066pt overflow in the coherent-category equivalence display. That display was split into two compact arrays, rebuilt, and explicitly inspected on page266. Both build records are retained.

All47 selected pages were actually inspected:8 by the root,12 by monodromy,12 by reduction/descent and15 by obstruction/crystals. ## Elliptic realization of the remaining height-two character pairs

The full B_height_two_elliptic_realization.tex proof and its rational-quaternion
certificate were independently read and checked by the root. The explicit
order-three and order-four conjugators, all division valuations, and the
Dic12 Hilbert90 normalization preserve the integral Tate lattice. The
Serre–Tate argument lifts the labelled semilinear special action and descends
only the generic elliptic curve; it does not descend the ramified good model.

Opened original Katz1981 author PDF at
https://web.math.princeton.edu/~nmk/old/serretatelocmod.pdf and read
Theorem1.2.1 with its complete proof, pp143–146 (including the preceding
lifting lemma used in full faithfulness). Original Lazard1955 at
https://numdam.org/item/10.24033/bsmf.1462.pdf was opened; TheoremIV and its
height-classification proof, pp270–271, were read with the arbitrary
algebraically closed field hypothesis. The direct EGAIII1 web PDF request
failed because the file exceeds the search tool's20MB limit; the retained
original full PDF/text was read at Theorems5.4.1 and5.4.5 with complete
proofs, pp156–158. This is actual original-text access, not reliance on the
failed URL. Theorem5.4.5 is correctly called a theorem.

## Exact norm-contact computations

The firstGF64 contact candidate passed source smoothness on all four
projective charts, but failed the global collision test: a further
collinear triple for translationsH and−H was detected by a nonunit
Groebner basis. Every matrix, input, local check, chart ideal and failure
output is retained. Two implementation errors before the geometry checks
are also retained: the generic SymPy finite-extension domain omitted its
associated-field interface, and a subclass name broke coefficient
conversion. The working interface keeps the actual extension class,
checks the modulus irreducible, and verifies all4096 products and all

No result was claimed from either failed execution or from rational-point
sampling. Subsequent candidates have separate numbered inputs and outputs.

## Completed coherent-crystal source and proof audit

The root subsequently read the entire ordinary coherent Noetherian proof,
Jiang's complete Appendix B (Definitions B.1--B.3, Remark B.4 and Theorem
B.5 with proof, pp.43--45), and reread Theorem2.1/Remark2.4 and
Proposition6.2/Theorem6.3 at the locators above. Original Stacks pages
https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/00MA,
https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/00MB,
https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/00IP, and
https://stacks.math.columbia.edu/tag/0H0L were opened and their complete
proofs read. Completion of finite modules, flatness of Noetherian
completion, Krull intersection and the complete-module model of formal
quasi-coherent objects were checked in their exact hypotheses.

The proof audit passed: the completed augmented Cech complex is
universally exact by a finite nilpotent filtration; finite Hom descends
ordinary morphisms; the flat formal-nerve projections lift a coherent
ordinary stratification to derived complete data; the nilpotent
filtration gives D<=0, and H0 gives an ordinary crystal. It does not
claim that the intermediate derived crystal is discrete. All-test
extension is proved by explicit finite-type graph neighbourhoods and
common refinements. The local Noetherian/coherent refinement is proved;
the broad counterexamples remain valid. Independent monodromy and
reduction/descent proof/source audits also passed.

## Ordinary surfaces with inseparable polarization

The root read the entire new proof and the previous theta-jet and nodal
incidence dependencies. The actual retained AV original was read at
Lemma4.44 and Proposition4.45 with proofs (p.67), Proposition5.22 with
proof (pp.80--81), Corollary7.17 and its duality context (p.104),
Corollary8.11 with proof (p.117), and Proposition8.19/Corollary8.20
with proofs (p.119). Theorem7.5 and its proof had already been opened
and checked in this run. Choosing the connected multiplicative
two-primary isotropic subgroup makes the dual principal-source isogeny
etale even when the degree is even. The norm separation and nodal
incidence arguments do not invert its degree. Arbitrary target
translation, not inversion on geometric two-torsion, fixes the exact
norm line bundle. The root exact arithmetic rerun passed in
0.014947520998248365 seconds and preserved the author's output.

## The discriminant form of the height-two gate

The full B-RD36 proof and its dependencies were checked. For an elliptic
curve with potential supersingular reduction in characteristic three,
12r=m delta and the faithful tame tangent multiplier imply
t=4/gcd(4,delta). The original Serre--Tate1968 printed page496 was read
from its retained original OCR; its first paragraph of Section2 states
the integral-j potential-good criterion and points to Deuring p.225.
The char3 Cartier coefficient and c4 congruence give the strict
supersingular inequality. The e=2 Cohen/Hensel field normal form,
discriminant bound23 and conversion of every remaining BT pair to an
elliptic pair were checked. The root certificate rerun passed in
0.04524370399667532 seconds, preserving the author's output. This is
a proved reformulation of the residual test, not a solution of it.

despite passing the four smooth-source charts and its affine pair tests.
Candidate03 passes all four source smoothness charts (3.2001894870008982
seconds) and all144 global collision ideals (74.52918819499973 seconds).
The144 checks consist of32 pair-tangency and112 triple-identification
checks, including the charts at infinity. Translation matrices are
certified by reduction of the actual rational addition identities on
the elliptic curve. At the two expected pair-support points the32nd
powers of all coordinate differences reduce to zero; all other pair
and triple ideals are the unit ideal. The independent direct local
series calculation verifies contact coefficient55 and mixed Cartier
value60. Monodromy independently inspected both programs and reran
all global checks to separate output files. These checks prove one
global simple self-tacnode witness with all other singularities nodes;
they do not solve the remaining two tangent-triple existence tests.

"ordinary abelian surface general member linear system ordinary curve
polarization characteristic p"; "coherent stratification infinitesimal
crystal flat locally finite presentation Noetherian equivalence Jiang";
and "supersingular product elliptic curves generic p-rank curves
bidegree characteristic 2". The returned results did not provide an
exact theorem matching these new formulations. The ordinary query
also returned Sannai--Tanaka's Frobenius-pushforward characterization;
the crystal query returned a different F-divided-bundle assertion and
general flatness references. No assertion is imported from those
search snippets or from secondary encyclopedia pages. The actual
proofs use the primary passages specified above and the retained
explicit calculations. No priority claim is made.

## Checkpoint 13: uniform rectangular ranks and residual character restrictions

The current outcome table supersedes earlier checkpoint gates. This is an interim checkpoint, marked INCOMPLETE. The next actual hourly review is due at12:50:40UTC; checkpoint packaging is not counted as mathematical movement.

## A.3: every ordinary embedding and every supersingular rectangle

For an ordinary abelian surface in characteristic two, the cube-embedding restriction has been removed. If M is any ample line bundle, D=chi(M), and n>=3, a geometric generic smooth member of |M^n| is ordinary, has genus Dn²+1, and has relative stable rank Dn²−1. For the embedding defined by M, the ambient degree bound is explicitly n>=max(3,reg(I_A)−1). The proof treats every principal multiple, including degree four jet exceptions and even-degree etale norm kernels, and then uses the proved ordinary principal-source norm construction. No statement about |M| or |M²| is inferred. The full proof passed root, monodromy and reduction/descent audits. The arithmetic certificate checks254 values of n and32512(n,D) identities; author runtime0.012083560999599285s and independent reduction/descent runtime0.013041352001891937s are retained.

The supersingular product result is now uniform in every product polarization type. On E² with E:y²+y=x³, for M=O(aO) external-product O(bO), L=M³, and every positive d,

    r_d = 9abd² − 1 − epsilon(ad) − epsilon(bd),

where epsilon(c)=1 for even c and0 for odd c. The genus is9abd²+1 and the relative coherent dimension is9abd²−1. The same complete-system rank applies to the ambient hypersurface family for d>=max(1,reg(I_A)−1). Thus both odd factors give rank9abd²−1, one even factor gives9abd²−2, and two even factors give9abd²−3. The earlier restriction on the two-adic valuation of the even factor is eliminated.

The infinite calculation is proved by a kernel sheaf, not by extrapolating finite matrices. For q divisible six, the sparse section in type(2q,3) gives a map V³→(det V)³, where V=F_*O(2qO). Its kernel is the trace-zero endomorphism bundle modified at exactly two reduced F4 points. The two global classes in End(V)/scalars are multiplication by source x and the invariant derivative. Requiring each to preserve the rank-one image line gives independent conditions at the two points; consequently the kernel has h0=2 and h1=0. The two kernel vectors are s and its first-factor derivative. Their map to the two-dimensional cokernel has matrix [[zeta,1],[zeta²,1]], determinant1. This proves both rankC and rankC² equal6q−2 for every q divisible six. Odd-degree etale norms and the earlier parity constructions give the complete formula above.

All source/base Frobenius conventions and both local elementary modifications were checked independently. Author, root and reduction/descent complete certificate runtimes are0.1617353309993632s,0.154388964001555s and0.1660513059978257s. The eight independently reconstructed matrices have q=6,12,18,24,30,48,96,192. The uniform proof uses none of those finite cases as an induction assumption. The discovery programs, rejected patterns, the nonsharp cases in q=3..96, and the initial SymPy-integer JSON serialization failure are all retained. The earlier dual-kernel diagnostic took0.30682451300526736s and is explicitly a finite diagnostic.

The unresolved A.3 scope is arbitrary smooth projective X and the remaining nonordinary inseparable polarization types outside these proved families.

## A.4: the degree-three Hasse divisor

The degree-three relative Hasse divisor of the fixed supersingular cubic product is geometrically irreducible and reduced. The proof uses the degree-nine norm map, the source factorization D²Q, the affine kernel action of order216, one self-tacnode witness and two tangent-triple witnesses representing the two unordered triple orbits. The exact orbits have sizes12 and72. The pair incidence is generically a P4 bundle and the triple incidence a P5 bundle; exceptional jet loci have no additional divisorial component.

The conductor calculation gives the full formula g(Z)−f(Z)=sum(g(C_i)−f(C_i))+sum(delta_x−r_x+1). This proves that every generic collision divisor maps into the target Hasse divisor. At the generic source determinant divisor the graph cohomology is Frobenius-unit over the local ring, so its pullback multiplicity is exactly two. The three local bridge criteria place the source cubic and all collision factors in one target component. Any additional target component would pull back with degree at most six, contradicting the norm pullback degree9*deg(H). A smooth Hasse point proves reducedness.

Every successful source was checked on all four projective charts, and every possible pair or triple collision was checked over the algebraic closure by exact ideal certificates. The first two failed contact witnesses are retained as failures. The two successful tangent-triple collision checks took72.36891810200177s and69.24018129799515s; independent reruns took76.3690687260023s and74.86146398200071s. The complete local and source-smoothness outputs, including actual runtimes, remain beside the programs. The root kernel-orbit rerun took0.006542789000377525s, and the three main-incidence ranks are exactly6,6,6.

The degree-three full group GL80(F2) was already proved by a separate certificate. This new divisor theorem does not imply a uniform higher-degree full-image theorem.

The uniform contact-source existence gate has also been closed. For every translation with both elliptic coordinates nonzero, an explicit irreducible Artin–Schreier coefficient family supplies a smooth global source with a simple prescribed contact and unequal values of its extra Cartier-killed form. In the nonflex case the local coefficient is T(c⁴+c). The full source-smoothness proof classifies all singularity incidences in its P4 contact slice. The degree54 full discriminant restricts with the four forced factors F³T³h³v² and one remaining irreducible factor of total degree43. The irreducible degree3 Hasse cubic cannot be that factor, so its generic source is globally smooth. The local and global proofs passed a complete root audit; the exact universal local certificate passed in0.34270756500336574s (author) and0.36782622399914544s (root). The program explicitly tests local identities only; the global conclusion comes from the written discriminant proof. Further collisions under a prescribed norm map and uniform tangent-triple bridges remain genuine unresolved gates.

## B.4: scalar residual exclusion and a smaller cyclic-four gate

At p=3,e=2,h=2, the least index remains in{2,3}: 81-torsion suffices, and the undecided test is27-torsion. The new scalar exclusion is already valid for9-torsion. After a simultaneous quadratic twist, a hypothetical remaining27 comparison has residual image either nontrivial unipotent C3 or irreducible tame C4.

The scalar proof reads Fisher's original full-level Hesse family and Theorem4.3. The reverse equation has the essential factor3. Its correct valuation shift is two modulo three, and it leaves two opposite-character valuation patterns, B/A and D/E. Neither is excluded merely by valuations. Each is excluded by an exact unit calculation: a required cube congruent to one would have deviation valuation one, whereas any such cube has deviation valuation at least three at absolute ramification two. The direct congruence, arbitrary Weil-pairing multiplier, simultaneous quadratic twist and residual subgroup classification are all included. The earlier unpromoted proof that omitted the factor3 is explicitly withdrawn and retained.

The original printed page9 was actually viewed by the root and the independent monodromy reviewer; equation(11) was checked visually. The root also read the full theorem proof, Section2 and Lemma4.2, and checked the derivation of each used condition. The corrected author certificate took0.0822893020012998s; root and monodromy reruns took0.06197141099983128s and0.09311267000157386s. All outputs preserve the author record.

For residual image C4, the projective level-three parameter obeys sigma(t)=(t+2)/(t−1) over the tame quadratic subfield. Thus Norm(t−1)=3. The resulting residue calculation gives v_K(j)=6. Identifying the actual marked quadratic twist, rather than only its j-invariant, gives30 congruent2v_K(Delta)+6 modulo12; hence6 divides v_K(Delta). Its special tame quotient has order one or two, so this residual case can occur in the remaining test only with individual special-inertia orders3 and6 and joint order18. Every joint-order36 test now has residual C3 after the common twist. The C4 proof passed root and obstruction/crystal full audits; its exact author/root/independent runtimes are0.07729700100026093s,0.1112131780027994s and0.10795447899727151s.

Neither residual alternative has yet been shown to exist or not exist at27-torsion. A proposed stronger logarithm estimate must control the good quotient's thickness; the original targets alone do not justify applying the existing saturation argument. That unresolved step is recorded rather than silently assumed.

## Proved restrictions after checkpoint13

For mixed elliptic products E_ord×E_ss in characteristic two and the cube of an arbitrary product polarization of type(a,b), the geometric generic relative stable rank is9abd²−1−epsilon(ad) for everyd>=2. The same formula holds atd1 unlessa is even andb=1. The full geometric proof uses arbitrary-degree ordinary etale sources, odd supersingular etale sources, the already proved cubic nodal-norm theorem and a transverse odd-plus-odd union. The even ordinary factor supplies the global additional Cartier kernel. Root and monodromy complete proof audits passed; the original AV5.20–5.22 and5.26 passages were read. The author/root arithmetic runtimes are0.03129147599975113s and0.03777776699280366s. This is an infinite-family theorem, not an extrapolation from the retained identities.

B39 first narrows a normalized unipotent residual comparison: the common three-division field is generated by a cube root of a noncube unit; the opposite tame orders are4 and2, with j-valuations3 and>=6. Every nonunit cube class is excluded by the exact reverse/direct unit deviations4,5,7 over the cubic extension. The scalar-unit depth proof is given explicitly by Hensel's equation w³+w+pi*w²=b. ### A.3: mixed surfaces beyond products

For a mixed abelian surface in characteristic two, write a(M) for the index of its polarization on the rank-one etale2-adic Tate module. Integral Dieudonne duality gives two paired elementary-divisor indices a(M),b(M), with v2(chi(M))=a(M)+b(M). The dual polarization chi(M)lambda_M^(-1) is integral, including inseparable polarizations. A prescribed etale principal norm source of degree chi(M) exists exactly when a(M)=0. In this class, for L=M³ and every d>=1, the generic relative stable rank is9chi(M)d²−1 in odd degree and9chi(M)d²−2 in even degree. The proof does not assume a product decomposition or odd chi(M). An even-degree Prym upper bound is also proved for every mixed polarization. The a(M)>0 class remains outside this theorem.

Root and monodromy complete independent proof/source/code audits passed. Oda's original integral skew Riemann form, including printed100–101 actually viewed, supplies the alternating lattice even in characteristic two; the inference uses W(k) being torsion-free. Exact finite-flat descent and duality sources were read. The author/root/monodromy certificate times are0.04614707099972293s,0.049978896997345146s and0.03037099799985299s. Universal block identities and289 index pairs,1024 odd norms and1024 even unions are retained; they supplement the geometric proof.

### A.4: uniform mixed contact with all other collisions excluded

For every finite odd subgroup of the fixed supersingular elliptic product whose two coordinate projections are injective, and every nonzero kernel difference, there is a globally smooth cubic source on its Hasse cubic whose norm image has exactly one simple self-tacnode and otherwise only ordinary nodes. The extra Cartier-kernel differential has unequal values at the identified tangents. The proof treats every moving pair and triple incidence, not merely a finite list of torsion points. Smooth coefficient-plane cubics give integral moving covers; explicit etale points handle nonflex cases, and the flex case has constant contact length3 and odd moving degree15. Triple limits exclude all remaining identifications. Irreducibility of the full contact family and the finite-flat length3 cluster argument combine these exclusions with source smoothness.

All three independent full proof audits passed. The final universal algebra certificate has author runtime0.19226371099648532s, root0.18832970500079682s and obstruction/crystal0.16412100700108567s. The first author program/output and the first root output are also retained. The final version replaces a tautological terminal expression check with actual source and translated branch substitutions; the leading coefficient is T+vF/h. The previously retained six-jet certificate places the global contact03 witness in the main incidence. Uniform tangent-triple bridges remain the current norm-family gate. No uniform higher-degree full-monodromy result is claimed.

### B.4: the smallest odd-prime height-two case is sharp

Theorem B-RD42 proves nu_min(3,2,2)=2 for the full permitted height-two class:27-torsion determines the requested character on prime-to-three inertia, whereas the explicit9-torsion ambiguity proves sharpness. Every remaining C4 pair normalizes to M_b:y²=x³−3x+3b and the pi-twist of M_b-prime. Their cyclic cubic good fields have Kummer classes determined by the residues of b up to sign. The reverse marked Fourier cocycle forces equal normalized unit residues; Fisher's reverse9 equation and an exact trace expansion then force b-prime²=b² in the residue field. Thus both curves have a common good extension of degree6, contradicting the previously necessary joint order18. This excludes the final branch.

## Checkpoint 15 work: all odd supersingular-product groups

The principal advance is a complete full-image theorem for every odd degree on the fixed supersingular product E² in characteristic two, with E:y²+y=x³ and its (3,3) embedding. The geometric and arithmetic groups are GL_(9d²−1)(F2) for every odd d>=1. The uniform even-degree and arbitrary-X problems remain open.

## Uniform tangent triples and odd-degree component allocation

The new third-value source-discriminant lemma proves that every required third-value hyperplane contains globally smooth sources with both source Hasse factors nonzero. A normalized-row construction gives simple contact of two branches and a transverse third, on the same irreducible rank-five incidence. The projected value map, its degree-two pencil poles, and the order-three pole-cancellation exception then exclude every other kernel collision uniformly. The cyclic three-element case uses the previously completely certified collinear witness only inside its subgroup, while the new incidence proof excludes all outside collisions. Root, reduction/descent and obstruction/crystal complete audits all passed.

The arbitrary-odd-degree assembly proves that the norm pullback has only the source cubic factors and pair/triple collision factors; the determinant factor D has multiplicity exactly two. Mixed-contact and tangent-triple smooth points assign all collision factors and Q to one target Hasse component. A further component would pull back to degree at most6, contradicting d²>=9. A sharp-source tacnode gives generic stable rank loss one. The relative coherent bundle is Omega1 on projective space, so determinant allocation and the exact absence of odd-degree invariant covectors give irreducibility. Purity and local Lang theory give transvection generation. The already proved actual natural GL8 block excludes every form-preserving alternative in Kantor–Seress Theorem3.6, original printedp22, which was actually viewed again by all auditors. This proves the full group without a finite-degree extrapolation.

## Dyadic eight-torsion ambiguity

B43 gives an explicit quotient of an elliptic curve with a rational point of order8 over the unramified quadratic extension of Q2, together with its exact Miller-function Kummer class q=−4lambda/3. Its8-torsion agrees with a Tate group, while the special-inertia characters differ on an order-three quotient element. The complete curve, point multiples, invariant calculations and Kummer identity are checked in retained exact programs. The lower bound nu_min(2,e,h)>=3 holds for3 not dividing e and h>=2. No16-torsion sufficiency is claimed.

## Additional mixed-product rank work

A fixed (6,3) section on every ordinary-times-supersingular product has a16minor of its twice-iterated dual Frobenius matrix equal to c^5, where c is the nonzero ordinary Weierstrass parameter. The complete p-basis identities and tensor Cartier matrix are derived symbolically. The exact search retains all six trials, including unsuccessful vectors; the independent verifier retains its initial software-domain coercion failure and corrected monic pseudo-remainder run. A proposed arbitrary-degree ordinary-axis norm transfer was invalid: it gives class(6,3n), whereas the required class(6n,3) comes from the supersingular axis and requires n odd for etaleness. The corrected nodal-incidence argument is written and independently audited. The current outcome table records promotion only after both seed and norm files have actually passed their independent audits.

The manuscript scans remain unavailable, and the transcription limits remain unchanged. The work is AI-produced by GPT-6 Astra (OpenAI, ChatGPT Pro mode), with no priority claim. The final numbered-statement, build, event and actual visual-inspection counts are recorded below after verification.

## Final checkpoint verification

The complete paper has369 pages and2,714,802 bytes. All32 selected pages were actually viewed, eight each by the root and three independent reviewers. The selection includes every new subsection, earlier samples, both overflow locations and the final bibliography. All displayed matrices, coefficient tables, formulas, paths, glyphs, margins and page transitions are clear.

There were three build attempts. The first stopped because a blank paragraph lay inside the local-type matrix display; its full merged input, source fragment, log and console are retained in outputs/failed_checkpoint15_build. The failing build exits before recording a runtime, so none is invented. The first successful build took39.461 seconds and reported one20.42859pt runtime-line overflow plus the existing0.41342pt line. Shortening that lead-in repaired the larger overflow. The final successful build took39.115 seconds and has one0.41342pt line, visually harmless, with zero boxes above2pt and no undefined references, citations or missing glyphs. The before/after counts above2pt are1 and0.

There are414 numbered statements, a net addition of19 since checkpoint14. The23 new identifiers consist of those19 additions and four earlier scope remarks whose content-derived identifiers changed when proved forward references were added. Root read all four complete old/current passage pairs. The first identity utility incorrectly required unchanged titles even though two titles now describe proved consequences; its exact source and failure traceback are retained. The corrected explicit injective map is recorded in outputs/statement_identity_checkpoint15.json. Every new identifier has an explicit check event.

The inspected paper includes the fully promoted mixed(6,3) seed and corrected axis-transfer theorem. Their two independent complete proof/program audits passed, so the degree-one thin-strip gate is now only4|a,b1. It also includes the fully promoted uniform tangent triples, all-odd-degree full group theorem, and B43's explicit dyadic8-torsion ambiguity.

Further dyadic16 restrictions (B44/B45), an even-degree first-image sheaf reduction, and an ordinary Frobenius-bundle approach to the residual thin strip were written after the freeze. They remain separate candidate work outside this inspected paper and current outcome table. All executed programs and failures from those investigations are retained in the checkpoint's changed working files; their existence is not a claim of promotion.

The bibliography currently contains142 entries and the source census143 rows, including the unused Hua witness. The final merged registers contain the exact check-event and exported-program counts. The positive-section divisor and normalized-cover conventions are derived directly in this dossier; a sign/convention discrepancy in the printed iteration formulas is explicitly recorded for the candidate threefold. Used for exact differentials and Tango/Raynaud construction; the root-cover very-ampleness upgrade and restriction kernels are proved separately. |
